lang-check corrections — Sesotho

0 decided
515 proposed corrections (697 of them South African orthography, from a re-check of all 1078 st bank lines). Only lines with strong corpus evidence are here; the other 35 triaged lines stay in review/lang-check-triage-st.md for a native reader. Red = current token, green = proposed. Nothing changes in the banks until the export is applied. Decisions persist in this browser.
ST-P1 · witty.cold[80] · triage · conf 1.00
EN: Somebody's driving home right now with the heater on full and the seat warmer as a bonus.
now: Motho o khannela hae hona joale ka heater e tletseng le seat warmer e le bonus.
proposed: Motho o kgannela hae hona jwale ka heater e tletseng le seat warmer e le bonus.
  • joale → jwale (pack ban) — banned by the st pack: 'joale' is banned by the st language pack (Lesotho orthography; house standard is South African Sesotho (Al 2026-09-06; jwale 907× vs joale 85×)) — house form 'jwale'; note the corpora attest 'joale' 85×
  • khannela → kgannela (orthography) — SA form kgannela: 3× leipzig/wiki/dictionary — “Bala ka Lynne le ntja ya hae Famous mohla ba thusang ho kgannela dikgomo tsa Andre tse lahlehileng, lapeng.” (Leipzig sot_community_2017 #718) · Lesotho form 0×

  • joalo → jwalo (pack ban) — banned by the st pack: 'joalo' is banned by the st language pack (Lesotho orthography; house standard is South African Sesotho (Al 2026-09-06; jwalo 2040× vs joalo 249× in the SA corpora)) — house form 'jwalo'; note the corpora attest 'joalo' 249×
  • joalo → jwalo (orthography) — SA form jwalo: 2040× leipzig/nchlt/morph/wiki/constitution/dictionary — “Ke kahoo re reng ho lla ho ya re lokela, empa le ha ho le jwalo tshepo e teng hobane ba kene hae mme ba tla phomola thab” (Leipzig sot_community_2017 #4936) · Lesotho form 249×
